# DSS dataset mapping, cache, and upload manifest

This manifest records the dataset-level decisions used by the DSS recipes in
`examples/multimodal/v3_baseline_dss`. It was generated from the active YAML
include graphs, `v14_16k_49k_omni_262k_actions.csv`, and the local cache at
`/home/svc-dss/cache/nemotron` on 2026-07-23.

> **Dated snapshot:** Mapping tables and cache columns below describe the
> 2026-07-23 observation. Subsequent upload/cache operations are recorded in
> `DSS_OPERATIONS_LOG.csv`, and the repeatable conversion procedure is in
> `DSS_MIGRATION_RUNBOOK.md`. At migration closure on 2026-07-24, all 237
> initially missing active cache entries and all five post-mapping 49K
> datasets were cached locally. The DSS-team restart cached the original
> MMLongBench media dataset, so the user-owned fallback is not used.

> **2026-07-28 correction addendum:** The tables below remain a historical
> 2026-07-23 snapshot. The active recipes now use exact JSONL subpaths for 201
> unique primary DSS references, use the prepared `v1` JSONLs for text safety,
> image safety, BenchFit, and Dense OCR, and use indexed
> `dss://image_shards@v0` media access for BenchFit and Dense OCR. Seven
> prefixed-media cases use named auxiliaries and `aux_data_prefixes`. No active
> recipe was changed to point directly at an absolute cache or Lustre path.
> The source mapping registry and its generated normalized form were updated
> to match these active references.

The runtime media-prefix corrections are:

| Record prefix | Named auxiliary | DSS media reference | Active occurrences |
|---|---|---|---:|
| `arxiv_chart_alignment/` | `arxiv_chart_alignment` | `filesystem+dss://arxiv_chart_alignment@v0` | 2 |
| `nonarxiv_chart/` | `nonarxiv_chart` | `filesystem+dss://nonarxiv_chart@v0` | 2 |
| `code2Img/` | `charxiv_code2img` | `filesystem+dss://charxiv_code2img@v0` | 1 |
| `train1/png/` | `figureqa` | `filesystem+dss://internvl@v0/image_data/figureqa/train1/png` | 1 |
| `hw_math/` | `haoweilai_hw_math` | `filesystem+dss://haoweilai_hw_math@v0` | 1 |

## How to read this document

- **Primary** is the training JSONL/dataset referenced by `path`.
- **Auxiliary** is the media root referenced by `aux.media_source`.
- **Cached** means a directory with that dataset name currently exists under
  `/home/svc-dss/cache/nemotron`. It does not mean the dataset is also in every other cluster cache.
- A mapping is **exact reference** when the active DSS URL occurs in the
  recipe-specific CSV row. **Same path, newer snapshot** ignores only the
  DSS snapshot version; it is used for one 16K primary reference.
- **Inherited/no CSV row** means the DSS reference came from the colleague-authored
  DSS snapshot but no source row for that dataset name exists in the mapping CSV.
- The three post-mapping leaves were inactive in this dated snapshot. They
  became active after their caches completed on 2026-07-24.

## Summary of the 2026-07-23 snapshot

| Recipe | Active YAMLs | Active leaf entries | Unique DSS datasets | Cached locally | Need caching |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| 16K web | 33 | 845 | 1090 | 958 | 132 |
| 16K web, no ultra text | 32 | 766 | 1011 | 879 | 132 |
| 49K video | 5 | 71 | 82 | 6 | 76 |
| MMLongBench ultra-long | 20 | 19 | 29 | 0 | 29 |

The no-ultra recipe reuses the 16K mappings and removes the active
`ultra_txt_materialized_0407_median_le_16384.yaml` include. Its mapping
table is therefore represented as a delta after the main 16K table.

## Datasets that need local caching

On 2026-07-23 there were 237 unique active DSS datasets without a directory in
`/home/svc-dss/cache/nemotron`. The historical table below is deduplicated
across all four entry points; it is retained to document the submitted cache
set, not to represent current cache state.

Cache one dataset with:

```bash
NVDATASET_GROUPID=omni_vision \
NVDATASET_CACHE_DIR=/home/svc-dss/cache/nemotron \
edt cache DATASET_NAME \
  --dest-cluster-name oci-hsg-cs-001 \
  --no-wait
```

No cache jobs were submitted while producing this manifest.
